### Proposal, sharing & export

**Verdict.** Behind, badly. The content is better — the only honest provenance story in this
comparison, a narrative built from real project data, a PROVISIONAL stamp that survives Print — and
none of it matters, because **the document cannot leave the building** and the price on it is wrong.

#### What cannot leave the building

| | Status | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| **A PDF file** | **None.** Export is `window.print()`. No pdfmake / jspdf / react-pdf / puppeteer in package.json | `screens/ProposalView.tsx:144` |
| **A filename** | Every proposal saves as **"Solar App.pdf"** with the app URL printed across every sheet by the browser's own header/footer — `/proposal` is a client component and cannot export its own metadata | `src/app/layout.tsx:6-9`, `src/app/(studio)/proposal/page.tsx` |
| **Excel** | **None.** CSV only | `screens/Step9Bom/index.tsx:154-155`; no xlsx library |
| **GLB** | **None.** `grep GLTFExporter` across the feature tree → 0 (we only *import* glTF via drei `useGLTF`). Their `.glb` export runs from Step 2, Step 5, the shared viewer and the project-list row menu, with per-category toggles and texture quality | `three/ObstructionMesh.tsx` |
| **WhatsApp** | **None.** `whatsapp\|wa.me\|mailto:\|sms:\|navigator.share` across the feature tree → **0 hits**. `customerPhone` is captured, declared *"improvement: proposal delivery"*, and read by nothing. There is no `customerEmail` field at all | `screens/Step1Setup.tsx:100-103`, `types.ts:26` |
| **The share link** | **Dead for every recipient.** `ShareViewer` looks the project up in the **viewing device's** localStorage; the file admits it. We still print the URL, a real scannable QR of it, and a 10.5 px line reading *"Viewable on the device this design was created on."* No expiry, no revocation (`grep expire\|revoke` in `types.ts` → 0) | `screens/ShareViewer.tsx:9,27`, `ProposalView.tsx:96,204-213` |
| **The price** | **Ignores the discount.** `designFp` serialises `pricing.marginPct` and not `pricing.discount`, so the memo key does not change and the warm cache is returned. Reproduced live on the 8-panel fixture: truth `{total 249,183 · discount 26,136 · payback 2.9}`, what prints `{total 276,871 · discount 0 · payback 3.3}`. **The CSV is computed fresh and is correct — the customer document is not, from the same design** | `lib/fingerprints.ts:183`, `lib/bom.ts:165-167` |

Two more that belong in the same breath: a printable GST-inclusive quote can be issued at the
hardcoded **12% default margin** from Step 7, skipping Steps 8 and 9 entirely
(`Step7Proposal.tsx:366-376`, `data/pricebook.ts:152`, `store/store.tsx:142`) — and the wizard gate we
claim covers **wizard routes only**: `router.ts:49-51` exposes `/proposal` and `/bom` ungated and
`Dashboard.tsx:278` navigates straight there from any project's row menu. `review.issuable` is
computed (`lib/review.ts:37,161`) and consumed by **nothing but its own test**, so the panel prints
"Not ready to issue" directly above an enabled button that issues it.

| # | Gap | ResLink evidence | Ours today | Why it matters | E/P |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **Share via WhatsApp — delivery from inside the product** | Step 7 export menu: `Share via WhatsApp`, `Download PDF Report`, `Download Excel BOM`, `Copy 3D View Link`. WhatsApp is also their support channel, with a pre-filled message carrying step, project name, project id, phone and user id | 0 hits for `whatsapp\|wa.me\|mailto:\|sms:\|navigator.share`. `customerPhone` (`types.ts:26`, `Step1Setup.tsx:100-103`) is read by nothing. Delivery = `window.print()` then leave the app | **An Indian EPC closes on WhatsApp.** Ours is ~17 taps ending outside the product; theirs is 3. **The single highest-leverage missing feature in this dimension, and the phone number is already in the model** | S / **high** |
| 2 | **A real PDF file — generated, named, attachable** | `Generate Proposal` → `Calculating energy...` → `Generating PDF...` → `Uploading PDF...` → `Generating share link...` → `Proposal Generated`, with `View PDF` and `Edit Photos` | `window.print()` at `ProposalView.tsx:144`; no PDF dependency; `layout.tsx:6-9` `title: 'Solar App'` | No file to attach, no filename control, nothing to store or re-send. The artefact the customer receives is **named after our app**. It is also the prerequisite for WhatsApp and email delivery | M / **high** |
| 3 | **Download Excel BOM** | `Download Excel BOM` on the Step-7 menu and the shell; 12 columns under a header block with project name, customer and capacity | CSV only (`Step9Bom/index.tsx:154-155`). `lib/bom.ts:159-179` already produces their column set **and five more**, and apportions the discount pro-rata at `:165-167` | Indian procurement works in Excel; a CSV mangles ₹ formatting and cannot be handed to a supplier as-is. **We are one serialiser away — the columns and the maths already exist and are better than theirs** | S / med |
| 4 | **A server-minted, revocable, expiring share link that works on someone else's phone** | `/share/:shareId` public 3D viewer with solar access, Energy Report, 3D↔top toggle, satellite, external buildings, sun path, share and `Export 3D model (.glb)`; links expire or are revoked — *"This design link has expired or been revoked."* | `ShareViewer.tsx:9` reads the viewing device's localStorage; `:27` says so. `ProposalView.tsx:204-213` prints the URL **and a real QR** under a 10.5 px confession. Needs a backend the product does not have | L / **high** |

No Capture All, no capture-frame overlay | Four sequential full-screen 3D mounts on a phone in the sun, switched by the smallest targets in the product, on the step meant to be done in the field. **One tap should do it** | S / med |
| 6 | **Pre-capture scene toggles and a framing guide** | Before capturing you toggle `Solar access`, `Satellite on roofs`, `Nearby buildings`, `Realistic models`, and pan/rotate; *"This area will be captured"* shows the crop | The toggles **all exist** — Heat (`Scene3D.tsx:1143`), Around (`:1189-1198`), Sun path (`:1200-1207`), mesh↔map (`:1185-1187`) — but nothing surfaces them as a capture checklist and there is no capture-area overlay. Content is fixed by `initial={{date,hour,solarAccess}}` (`Step7Proposal.tsx:89`) | The user cannot see what will be in the frame before spending the capture, and **the four toggles that decide whether the picture sells are three levels into a radial menu** | S / med |
| 7 | **A named-stage generate pipeline with a finished state** | Four named stages → `Proposal Generated` → `View PDF` / `Edit Photos` | `Generate Proposal` is `patch({status:'proposal_ready'}); navigate('/proposal')` (`Step7Proposal.tsx:366-376`). No progress, no completion state, no artefact. We do have `Edit Photos` (`:363-365`) | "Generate" that instantly renders a web page reads as **nothing having happened**, and there is no moment where the EPC has A Thing to send. The named stages also explain the wait honestly | S / med |
| 8 | **.glb 3D model export with per-category toggles and texture quality** | `Export 3D model (.glb)` from Step 2, Step 5, the shared viewer and the project-list row menu; dialog toggles `Solar Panels`, `Roofs`, `Obstructions`, `Satellite Overlay`, plus `Texture Quality` and a copyable link | `grep GLTFExporter` → 0. Our download surface is SVG/PNG/DXF (`Step8Sld.tsx:45-62,180`) and CSV (`Step9Bom/index.tsx:154`) | **A .glb is how an EPC hands the model to an architect or the client's CAD team.** `GLTFExporter` ships inside the `three` package we already depend on — the exporter is nearly free; the toggles are the work | M / med |
| 9 | **Company logo as a company-level asset with crop & scale** | Step 0: company logo upload with crop & scale (max 5 MB, 12 × 6 cm, PNG/JPG) that lands on the proposal | `logoDataUrl` is **per project**, null by default (`types.ts:34`, `store/store.tsx:120`, `Step1Setup.tsx:260-261`) — the EPC re-uploads on every job — rendered once as a raw `<img maxHeight:46>` (`ProposalView.tsx:176-177`). `AppUser.companyName` (`types.ts:1275`) reaches only the dashboard; the footer hardcodes **`PAGE {num} · Generated by Solar Studio`** (`:549`) | **Our customer-facing document is branded with our product name**, and carries no logo at all on any job where the EPC forgot to re-upload. White-labelling is table stakes for a tool an EPC resells through | S / **high** |
| 10 | **Four project statuses in the list** | Filters: `Draft`, `In progress`, `Design ready`, `Proposal ready` | `type ProjectStatus = 'in_progress' \| 'proposal_ready'` (`types.ts:21`), both rendered at `Dashboard.tsx:344`. Nothing records sent / viewed / accepted / rejected | An EPC with 40 live quotes **cannot tell a finished design from one that has been sent**. Even inside the design tool they separate "design ready" from "proposal ready" | S / med |
| 11 | **Satellite overlay on/off in the shared viewer** | Shared viewer: "satellite overlay on/off"; step 5: `Hide/Show satellite overlay` | No satellite toggle. The closest is the mesh↔map view-mode switch (`Scene3D.tsx:1185-1187`), which swaps the ground **and** strips the sun path, surround relief and the time bar (`:1890`) | Small, but it is the toggle that makes a render look like a photo rather than a diagram, and **ours is welded to a mode switch that removes half the scene** | S / low |
| 12 | **OPEX/PPA proposals with live tenure, escalation and ROI** — *their separate Proposals product, not the design tool* | YouTube `OMXhWiapA_k`: "generate PPA proposals in minutes, adjust contract tenure and tariff escalation live, automatic ROI, present in the customer meeting" | We ship a four-mode financing table **inside** the design tool (`ProposalView.tsx:358-399`, `lib/financing.ts`) — but nothing is adjustable. Tenure/rate/discount come from `resolveRules().financing` with no UI (`financing.ts:55,74,89,103,111`); escalation is `const escalation = 3;` mid-function (`lib/finance.ts:44`) and printed to the customer as fact (`ProposalView.tsx:349`); PPA lifetime is `ppaAnnual × tenure` with no escalation and no degradation (`financing.ts:111`). `grep -w IRR` and `grep depreciation` → 0 files | **Their rep changes tenure in front of the customer and beats our number. Ours is a constant in a source file.** The model is built and reconciled to one quote total — it needs four inputs and an escalation term, not a rewrite | M / **high** |

**Two qualifiers this table needs.** (a) ResLink's design tool is one product in a four-product
suite — `Proposals`, `CRM`, `Projects`, `3D Design`, and their Step 10 is literally *"Done & Return
to Proposal"*. "Their design tool has no financials" is true; **"ResLink has no financials" is not.**
Our v1 scope is the whole funnel, and `grep -rni crm src/` → 0. (b) Their Energy Report has a
**second** entry point into generation — `Customize Proposal` / `Quick Generate` — so the report
itself closes the deal; ours ends at a Next button.

#### We already beat them at

| They have nothing like | Ours | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| **A customer narrative built only from real project data** | Every beat carries `facts:` — the raw project values it was built from, so a test can prove no number appeared from nowhere; sections with no real data are **omitted rather than padded**. **Do not throw this away** | `lib/proposal-narrative.ts:31`, `ProposalView.tsx:218-236` |
| **A provisional stamp that survives Print** | With `print`, `FreshnessBanner` keeps its class instead of `.no-print`, so a document printed while shading/strings/routes are stale **says so on paper**; mounted twice so the physical first sheet carries it | `components/FreshnessBanner.tsx:29,43`, `ProposalView.tsx:102,164` |
| **A pre-issue readiness review** | Four signals — electrical, design review, quantity confidence, imagery — each with a jump-to-the-step button | `lib/review.ts`, `Step7Proposal.tsx:187-229` |
| **Per-capture staleness, and the user chooses the cover** | Each capture stamped with the layout fingerprint it was taken against; an "Outdated — retake" badge on any capture the design has moved past; any capture can be promoted to Cover. Theirs ships `Retake` with no staleness detection | `lib/fingerprints.ts:334-343`, `Step7Proposal.tsx:262-289,320-348` |
| **A customer/engineering split with arithmetic that holds** | The engineering doc prints cost + margin% → taxable-before-discount − discount → taxable + GST = system cost; the customer doc prints the one final price. The in-file comment records that the short version went arithmetically false once GST went per-line | `ProposalView.tsx:433-457,460` |
| **Four financing modes reconciled to one quote total, inside the design tool** | Cash / Loan / Lease / PPA all derived from the same `fin.systemCostInr` and the same annual saving, so no financing view can disagree with the BOM | `lib/financing.ts:50-121`, `ProposalView.tsx:358-399` |
| **A real scannable QR of the share URL** | `qrcode` → inline SVG, self-contained, no external service, prints crisply. Theirs is copy-link only. *(The link it encodes is dead off-device — the mechanism is right, the backend is missing)* | `ProposalView.tsx:27-41,213` |
| **An electrical page inside the proposal** | Optional SLD page driven by the same `effectiveSld(project)` as the Step-8 sheet, so the two cannot print different ratings. Theirs is a separate step with its own exports | `ProposalView.tsx:468-513`, guard at `:92-93` |
| **Structural honesty on the customer document** | The engineering status label, `STRUCTURE_DISCLAIMER`, the state's wind-zone note and the engineer's notes all print on the pricing page. They sell "Detailed Structure Analysis" as a paid tier with no disclaimer travelling with the output | `ProposalView.tsx:400-409` |
| **Losses, monsoon-split monthly chart and stated irradiance provenance on the customer document** | Monthly bars coloured by `monsoonMonths`, and a printed line reading either *"Monthly shape from measured PVGIS climate (real monsoon variation)"* or *"built-in seasonal model (estimate, ±10%)"*. Theirs live in the step-5 report, not the proposal | `ProposalView.tsx:291-321,305-309` |
| **Camera control in the shared viewer** | Top / Iso / Front / Back / Left / Right presets plus Fly, Isolate, Walk, Fullscreen, and distance / angle / area / height measurement. Theirs offers "switch 3D ↔ top view" | `three/Scene3D.tsx:1252-1297,1310-1333` |


---

## Gap register — 3D realism and UI

### 3D realism, objects & views

**Our renderer is a generation ahead of theirs. Our objects and our phone performance are a generation behind.**

The renderer is not the problem. We ship ACES filmic tone mapping in an HDR composer, an IBL environment, N8AO ambient occlusion, SMAA, clearcoat physical glass, module faces drawn from the datasheet, a 4096 shadow map, a live Preetham sky and real Google 3D-Tiles / DSM surroundings (`three/ScenePost.tsx:33-45`, `three/Scene3D.tsx:1409-1411`, `:2746-2770`, `three/textures.ts:214-224`, `:40-171`, `three/Scene3D.tsx:2801-2811`, `:2838-2839`, `three/RealSurround.tsx:274-297`). ResLink has one directional light, a `shadowMapSize: 2048`, plain `meshStandardMaterial`, and — quoted from their bundle read — "No tone mapping, no env map / IBL, no PMREM, no post-processing stack found in the app bundle."

We lose on two things, and both are the things the customer actually sees.

**Objects.** Of 11 `ObstructionType` values (`types.ts:221-231`), **6 have a GLB** (`three/ObstructionMesh.tsx:60-67`: tank, tree, chimney, dish, solar water heater, turbine vent). `building` is a tinted box (`:187-206`), `windmill` is procedural and its asset folder `public/models/obstructions/windmill/` **holds only a `.gitkeep`**, and `elevated`, `ladder` and `other` fall through to a plain grey `boxGeometry` in `#7d8590` (`three/ObstructionMesh.tsx:255-270`). A ladder renders as a grey box. Against that: **17 obstruction types plus 4 skylight sub-shapes** (Flat glass / Pitched / Tilted / Arched) and **10 preloaded GLB props** — `tree.gltf`, `water_tank.glb`, `chimney.glb`, `street_light.glb`, `fence.glb`, `LA.glb`, `solar_water_heater.glb`, `ladder.glb`, `inverter.glb`, `cars.glb`. We have no AC outdoor unit, no skylight, no light pole, no transformer pad, no fence, no keep-out object, no cars — on Indian rooftops that all exist.

**Phone performance.** We ship **168.7 MB / 4,771,015 triangles** of unoptimised GLBs (measured from the GLB JSON chunks: turbine-vent 39.2 MB / 1,128,385 tris; tree 34.7 / 1,141,083; chimney 34.5 / 929,969; solar-wh 26.8 / 692,340; tank 21.2 / 623,983; dish 12.2 / 255,255). Five of six carry no `extensionsUsed` at all — no Draco, no meshopt, no KTX2, raw float32 geometry and raw JPEGs up to 3,334 KB, against `public/models/obstructions/README.md`'s own "ideally 1K or 2K" rule. For scale, the entire 200-module array is ~2,400 triangles (`three/PanelsInstanced.tsx:135-152`). And ambient occlusion — the one effect that stops a table looking like it floats — **is switched off by regex on 100 % of the target devices by design**: `three/ScenePost.tsx:20-25` returns low-power for any UA matching `/Android|iPhone|iPad|Mobile/i`, including an iPhone 15 Pro.

| Gap — what they have | Their evidence | Ours today (file:line) | Why it matters | Cost · payoff |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **A plain-models escape hatch** — one tap swaps every GLB prop for a primitive | `Switch to realistic models` / `Switch to plain models`; "Real GLB props, preloaded… Plain mode = primitive boxes" (inventory §3D) | No such toggle. `three/ObstructionMesh.tsx:60-67` MODEL_BY_TYPE is unconditional; `three/Scene3D.tsx:2387` `useWarmObstructionAssets(...)` prefetches every type in the project the moment 3D opens. Measured: tank + turbine vent + dish + solar water heater = **99.4 MB / 2.74 M tris before the first frame**; add one tree and it is 134.1 MB / 3.88 M. Our only view switch (`:1181-1187` Map/Mesh) drops **zero** GLBs | 99 MB is roughly three minutes of download on tier-2 4G with no way to opt out. **This is the single biggest reason their 3D works on a phone and ours does not** | S · high |
| **Compressed, decimated props** | Ten props served from `/design/models/`, all preloaded at boot with no reported cost (inventory §3D) | 168.7 MB / 4,771,015 tris, no Draco/meshopt/KTX2 on five of six files. A 0.4 m turbine vent carries **470× the triangles of the whole 200-module design** | gltf-transform (Draco/meshopt + simplify to ~15k tris + KTX2 at 1K) takes 168.7 MB to well under 5 MB. **The Draco decoder is already wired** at `three/RealSurround.tsx:58-59`, so `useGLTF(url, true)` needs no new infrastructure. Nothing else on this list is worth doing on mobile first | S · high |
| **WebGL context-loss recovery** | "They handle `webglcontextlost` / `webglcontextrestored` and show `\"Recovering 3D view...\"`" (inventory §0) | `grep -rn 'webglcontextlost\|contextrestored' src/` → **ZERO**. `three/Scene3D.tsx:1400-1412` creates the Canvas with `preserveDrawingBuffer: true` and no loss handler | Context loss is routine on Android when a tab holds ~100 MB of textures — the exact state we put it in. **Today the 3D view goes permanently black with no message and no recovery**; the user reloads and re-downloads the GLBs | S · high |
| **Scene telemetry — they know how their 3D runs on their users' phones** | "GPU renderer/vendor string, dpr, fps, worst frame gap, draw calls, triangles, texture count — sampled every 2 s and shipped" (inventory §0) | `grep -rn 'drawCalls\|renderer.info\|gl.info\|WEBGL_debug_renderer' src/` → **ZERO**. `three/Scene3D.tsx` has only a developer `window.__three` handle | Every quality decision below is currently a guess. **They can answer "what does a Redmi Note render at" from data**; we argue about it | S · high |
| **A measured performance tier instead of a User-Agent guess** | Their whole rig — one directional light + 2048 map + standard materials — is a budget that runs everywhere by construction, confirmed by per-frame telemetry | `three/ScenePost.tsx:20-25` `lowPower()` is a UA regex or `hardwareConcurrency <= 4`; `:31-38` then ships SMAA only and **deletes N8AO**. No `AdaptiveDpr`, no `PerformanceMonitor`, no `useDetectGPU` anywhere (grep: zero). `three/Scene3D.tsx:1402` runs `frameloop='always'` with `dpr={[1,1.5]}` fixed | A flagship Android renders our scene beautifully and **we refuse to let it**; a 2019 budget phone melts and we do nothing. `invalidate()` is already correctly called (`three/RealSurround.tsx:361,484,502`, `three/SurroundRelief.tsx:157,173`) — **demand mode is half-built and currently a no-op** | M · high |
| **More object types with real meshes** — ladder, lightning arrester, street light, fence, cars, inverter | 17 types + Skylight's four sub-shapes; `ladder.glb`, `LA.glb`, `fence.glb`, `street_light.glb`, `cars.glb`, `inverter.glb`; `Ladder is placed by two taps` (inventory §2, §3D) | 11 types, 6 with a GLB; `elevated`/`ladder`/`other` → grey box (`three/ObstructionMesh.tsx:255-270`); windmill's folder is a `.gitkeep` | An Indian rooftop the customer recognises has an AC outdoor unit, a ladder, a parapet LA and a Sintex tank. **Realism here is not shader work — it is having the right objects.** Corollary: our LAs, walkways and rails are already first-class project entities (`three/Scene3D.tsx:3353-3396`) that ResLink only draws in 2D — **we need meshes for data we already hold** | M · medium |
| **Satellite-overlay on/off as a user control** | 3D Preview panel: `Hide/Show satellite overlay` · `Hide/Show external buildings` · `Hide/Show annotations` · `Hide/Show sun path` · `Show/Hide mounting frame` (inventory §5) | Our halo (`three/Scene3D.tsx:1141-1354`) ships heatmap, access, labels, report, view mode, surround, sun path, electrical, wire, share, 6 presets, fly, isolate, walk, fullscreen and 4 measure tools — **but no satellite toggle**. The photo can only be removed by switching to Mesh (`:1181-1187`), which also swaps sky, ground and lighting | The aerial photo carries the site's **own** sun, its own shadows and often its own existing panels. An engineer reviewing our shadow simulation must be able to take it off without losing the sky. One boolean on the existing ground/photo materials | S · medium |
| **GLB export of the design**, with per-category toggles and a texture-quality setting | `Export 3D model (.glb)`; toggles `Solar Panels` / `Roofs` / `Obstructions` / `Satellite Overlay` plus `Texture Quality` and a share link. Gated: `exportNotOnPlan: "3D export is not available on your plan. Upgrade to Pro to export 3D models."` | `grep -rn 'GLTFExporter' three/ lib/ screens/` → **ZERO**. We have a `share` halo item (`:1234`) but no model export | A C&I client's architect or structural consultant asks for the model. **They monetise it as a Pro feature.** three.js ships `GLTFExporter` and our scene graph is already grouped | M · medium |

**Also missing, and not in the eight above:**

- **A global mounting-frame toggle.** Theirs is one switch (`Show/Hide mounting frame`, inventory §5). Ours is reachable only by selecting a single table and entering structure edit — there is no "show me the steel under the whole array", though `three/Scene3D.tsx:3144-3150` renders it unconditionally and `lib/structure-view.ts:60-80` `partitionPanels` already handles ghost-everything. S.
- **View persistence and saved views — we have neither, and neither do they.** `three/Scene3D.tsx:444-455` recomputes every camera preset from the bounding sphere on **every mount**, so framing a roof, stepping back to 2D and reopening 3D drops you at the default iso, every time. There is no "the angle I show customers". ResLink's captured angle at least survives as a `Share 3D view` link. Persist the pose per project — **not** in `designFp`/`layoutFp` (`lib/structure-view.ts:8-10` states view state must never key a fingerprint). M.
- **Front / Back / Left / Right are not elevations.** They fly to a 40° perspective FOV on a `[0, 0.3, 1]` direction — looking down ~17°, near objects larger than far (`three/Scene3D.tsx:464-471`, `:454`). Print one and no dimension on it is measurable. No orthographic mode, no focal-length control. ResLink does not ship this either (their shared viewer offers only 3D ↔ top) — **but PVcase and SolarEdge ship true orthographic elevations, so this is us doing a market-standard thing worse than not doing it.** M.
- **Capture resolution.** Our proposal hero is `gl.domElement.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.85)` on the live canvas at `dpr={[1,1.5]}` (`three/Scene3D.tsx:1126-1129` → `screens/Step7Proposal.tsx:43-64`) — from a 390 px phone that is ~585 px wide, into a PDF that wants ~2480 px at A4/300 dpi. Theirs: `Tap to capture` · `Cover Image` · `Shadow Analysis` · `Shadow captures: {{captured}}/{{total}}` · `Capture All` · `Retake` · `Orbit to adjust angle`, with pre-shot toggles, and a `Texture Quality` setting on export. S.

**We already beat them at**

| What we ship that they do not | Evidence |
|---|---|
| **A real physically-based pipeline** — ACES filmic tone mapping in an HDR (HalfFloat) composer, N8AO ambient occlusion, SMAA | `three/ScenePost.tsx:33-45`, `three/Scene3D.tsx:1409-1411`. Their bundle: "No tone mapping, no env map / IBL, no PMREM, no post-processing stack." HalfFloat confirmed as the library default (`node_modules/@react-three/postprocessing/dist/index.js:328`) |
| **Image-based lighting at all** — every PBR surface has something to reflect | `three/Scene3D.tsx:2746-2770` — drei `<Environment resolution={128} frames={1}>` with three Lightformer rects. They have no environment map of any kind |
| **Clearcoat physical glass**, not plain standard material | `three/textures.ts:214-224` — `MeshPhysicalMaterial({ roughness: 0.08, clearcoat: 0.5, clearcoatRoughness: 0.06 })`. Theirs is `meshStandardMaterial` glass at `roughness .12 / metalness .55` — **glass is not 55 % metal**, and standard material has no clearcoat lobe |
| **The module face is drawn from the datasheet**, not a stock texture — cell count from module length, cell colour and busbar count from technology, half-cut centre gap, per-cell tone wobble, fingers and ribbons at true scale, per orientation | `three/textures.ts:40-42`, `:97-158`, `:161-171`. **Swap a 540 W Adani for a 585 W TOPCon and the picture changes correctly.** Nothing comparable in their inventory |
| **A 4096 shadow map** — double their resolution per axis, four times the texels | `three/Scene3D.tsx:2838-2839`; theirs `shadowMapSize: 2048`. **Caveat, and it is a real one: the frustum is fitted at `:2719` to bounds that exclude obstructions entirely (`three/scene-bounds.ts:22-56` reads roofs and panels only), so a tall neighbour outside the box casts no shadow in the picture while `lib/shading.ts` still counts it in the number.** Carry the caveat wherever this row is quoted |
| **Real streamed neighbourhood geometry** — Google 3D Tiles photomesh plus a DSM relief fallback — where their "external buildings" are hand-placed obstructions | `three/RealSurround.tsx:274-297`, `three/SurroundRelief.tsx:56-125`. Their inventory explicitly lists "No LIDAR or photogrammetry import". Note: Google's India tiles carry no buildings, so **the DSM relief is the live path here** |
| **The mounting structure is always drawn from a real parametric model** — rafters, purlins, rails, foundations, pedestals, leg plans | `three/Scene3D.tsx:3144-3150`, `three/StructEditPanel.tsx`, `three/LegPlanEditor.tsx`. Theirs is a `Show/Hide mounting frame` switch on a decoration; **ours is a billed engineering model that also drives the BOM** |
| **A live sky simulation, atmospheric haze and a night sky** | `three/Scene3D.tsx:2801-2811` (Preetham `<Sky>` on the real sun direction, `<Stars>` below the horizon), `:177-194` (`FogExp2`). **Nothing in their inventory renders a sky at all** |
| **Instanced modules** — draw calls do not scale with system size | `three/PanelsInstanced.tsx:131-152`, per-instance colour writes at `:216-227`. A 500-module C&I array is 3–4 draw calls |
| **Six camera presets, fly-to-selection, isolate, first-person walkthrough, fullscreen and four measurement tools in the 3D scene** | `three/Scene3D.tsx:1252-1341`, `three/Measure.tsx`. Their shared viewer offers only "switch 3D ↔ top view" |

### UI, mobile & platform

**We are badly behind, and not on ideas — on execution.** Our own binding `docs/DESIGN-SYSTEM.md` specifies a better mobile contract than ResLink ships, and the product violates almost every clause of it. Verified live at 390×780 on commit `3700f6b`:

| Phone failure | Measured | Cause |
|---|---|---|
| **The Next button is 240 px off-screen on all ten steps** | `clientWidth 390`, `header.scrollWidth 630`, Next at left 539 → right 630. Also off-screen: ft toggle (right 395), Save project (401→441), Save & go home (447→487), Help (493→533). Document overflow: 255 px Step 2, 265 px Step 6, 263 px Step 9, 102 px `/proposal` | `screens/Wizard.tsx:287-296` — `flex; justify-content: space-between`, no `flexWrap`, and **there is exactly one width media query in the entire repo** (`theme.css:1165`, `max-width: 520px`, and it only scales the radial menu) |
| **Zero touch-gesture code on the 2D canvas** | `grep -rnE 'onTouchStart\|onTouchMove\|onTouchEnd\|\.touches\|changedTouches\|pinch\|navigator\.vibrate' src` → **2 hits, both prose comments** | `components/SatCanvas.tsx:233` sets `touchAction:'none'` (killing native pinch) and replaces it with nothing; `:152` pans only on middle mouse; zoom is `onWheel` (`:180`) plus two 38 px buttons, one tooltipped "(scroll wheel)"; `dragRef` (`:97`) tracks a single pointer, so **pinch is impossible by construction** |
| **96–100 % of controls fail the 44 px minimum** | Step 2: 27 of 28. Step 6: 26 of 27. Step 9 (BOM): **234 of 234**. Smallest measured: Lock/Delete Roof at **25×15 px** (375 px² against the 1936 px² N2 requires) | `theme.css` — `.btn` 40, `.chip` 30, `.toggle` 42×24, `.tool-btn` 38, `.ctx-btn` 28, `.step-btn` 32. The `.tap-target` utility written for exactly this (`src/design/index.css:190-200`) is used **zero times** |
| **No error boundaries anywhere** | `grep -rn 'ErrorBoundary\|componentDidCatch' src` → **0** | One React error in 12,573 lines of screen code white-screens the whole app |
| **No loading, empty, error or offline states — N10 unmet on all 18 screens** | `aria-busy` → 0; `onLine` → 0 real hits; no skeleton component | `StudioClientLayout.tsx:143` returns `state.hydrated ? children : null` — **a blank first paint on every route**. `components/SatCanvas.tsx:236-250` renders the satellite base image with **no `onError`**, so lost signal on a rooftop draws a silent dark rectangle the user then traces a roof onto |

Beyond the phone, these are platform capabilities **we do not have at all**: four languages, offline project loading, in-app help that reaches a human, a guided tour, a real tutorial video, a boot watchdog, WebGL context-loss recovery, session replay, scene telemetry, and plan entitlements that degrade to read-only.

| Gap — what they have | Their evidence | Ours today (file:line) | Why it matters | Cost · payoff |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Touch gestures on the 2D drawing canvas** — pinch-zoom, one-finger pan, double-tap to close | `tapToPlace: "Tap to place vertices, double-tap to close"`; `NTS · {{sheet}} Landscape · Pinch/Scroll=Zoom · Drag=Pan · DblTap=Fit`; plus a courtesy guard `"Screen zoomed in" / "Pinch two fingers together to zoom back out."` | Zero touch code (above). In Step 6 every branch of `onCanvasDown` returns true (`screens/Step6Editor.tsx:1287-1322`) so `mode` is always `'custom'` and **the canvas cannot be panned by finger at all**; in Step 2 `panEnabled={!draft && ...}` (`:1250`) blocks panning while tracing, so a shed wider than the viewport cannot be traced | **This is the whole wedge.** Their pitch is "India's First Mobile-First 3D Solar Design & Proposal Software" and design→proposal "in under 60 seconds" from a phone. An engineer on a roof cannot move or zoom our satellite image. Every other mobile fix is cosmetic until this exists. **DESIGN-SYSTEM §7.2 already specifies the gesture table, and `three/Scene3D.tsx:1492-1496` already implements it for the 3D camera** — the pattern is 30 lines away in our own tree | M · high |
| **A full four-language product** (English, Hindi, Thai, Vietnamese), with the Devanagari face actually loaded | "four full dictionaries; the Hindi and Thai ones are complete, not stubs"; the tutorial video is wired in all four (`en vaO5sSTyWzc`, `hi B9nYKJgQFHE`, `th L9M5gzpKRBM`, `vi RskKyuXvvIM`) behind a language picker | No i18n framework — `grep -rni 'i18n\|useTranslation' src` → 0; only `toLocaleString('en-IN')`. `screens/Dashboard.tsx:34-42` ships a four-language picker whose own comment admits *"Only English is actually translated … not selectable"*, `hi/th/vi` hard-flagged `false`. **And the font never loads**: `src/design/tokens.css:121-122` references `var(--font-inter), var(--font-devanagari)` while `next/font` is never imported in `src/app/layout.tsx`, so both are undefined and the whole `font-family` on `.ds` is invalid at computed-value time | The customer reading a ₹3 lakh proposal in a tier-2 town reads Hindi. We print English at **8.5 px** (measured on `/proposal` at 390 px, 194 text nodes under 12 px). **Even if we translated tomorrow we would render Devanagari in a system fallback** | L · high |
| **Offline operation** — the project list loads without a network | `Offline`, `loadedFromCacheOffline: "Loaded from cache (offline)"`, plan cache `reslink_plan_cache_v1`, sync states `Saved to your account` / `On this device only` / `Saving…` | No service worker, no manifest, no `navigator.onLine` — `grep -rn 'serviceWorker\|manifest.json\|workbox\|next-pwa' src public` → 0. We do have a careful local-first store (`lib/persistence/repository.ts:1-7`) — **but with no SW the Next.js shell cannot boot without signal, so the data is there and unreachable** | Tier-2/3 rooftops have patchy 4G and stairwells have none. Their offline state is a shipped string; ours is a blank page. **We are one service worker away from beating them here, because our persistence layer is better than theirs** | M · high |
| **In-product support that reaches a human** — per-step Help, WhatsApp pre-filled with context, Email us, YouTube Tutorials | `Chat on WhatsApp`, `Email us`, `YouTube Tutorials`, `Quick tour`, `aboutStep: "About: {{step}}"` — and **the WhatsApp message is pre-filled with step, project name, project id, phone and user id** | A static help sheet only (`screens/Wizard.tsx:40` STEP_HELP, rendered at `:417-430`). `grep -rni 'whatsapp\|wa\.me\|mailto:\|tel:' src` → **exactly one hit, and it is a mock button on the token-reference page** (`src/app/design/page.tsx:52`). **The product cannot contact anyone** | WhatsApp is the support channel in Indian EPC, and **our own DESIGN-SYSTEM §1 names it as the primary channel**. Their pre-filled message opens support on the exact project at the exact step. **Cheapest credibility item on the list** | S · high |
| **A guided tour with "Don't show again", and a real in-app tutorial video** | Guided tour on the header tools (Save & go home, Save your work, Need help?) with `Don't show again`; `Tutorial: Getting Started` on Step 0 in a `youtube-nocookie.com` iframe with a language picker | Zero onboarding — no tour, no coachmarks, no "don't show again" anywhere. `screens/Step1Setup.tsx:44-71` **copies their string verbatim** onto a 170 px gradient `<div>` with a play icon and **no onClick, no href, no role** — a control that does nothing, which DESIGN-SYSTEM §11 bans by name, burning **22 % of a 780 px phone viewport above the fold on the very first screen** | A first-time installer opening a 10-step wizard with no tour and a fake video button learns nothing and leaves. **We already wrote the help copy; we never made it discoverable or made the video real** | S · medium |
| **Crash resilience** — a boot watchdog with self-repair, plus WebGL context-loss recovery and a per-step error boundary | "Boot guard v3 in index.html: watchdog + self-repair", deliberately above the fonts stylesheet so a hung CDN cannot block boot; `"Recovering 3D view..."`; the BOM step's own boundary — *"Your design is safe — nothing here has changed it. On theirs you see "Recovering 3D view…"; on ours the tab dies and **the user loses the site visit** | S · high |
| **Telemetry and session replay** | GPU/dpr/fps/worst-frame-gap/draw-calls/triangles/texture-count every 2 s; routes `"/replay/:sessionId"`, chunks `ReplayPlayerPage-*.js`, `replayFrames-*.js`, `replayBoot-*.js`; Sentry + PostHog in the stack | `grep -rni 'sentry\|posthog\|clarity\|analytics' src` → **0** | We are guessing about performance on the exact device class we sell to. **They know which GPU dropped which frame on which step.** Every installer bug report we get is unreproducible | M · medium |

It is also `role="dialog" aria-modal="true"` with a dimming backdrop (`ui.tsx:87-88`), **so on Step 3 you type a water tank's height while the tank is hidden behind the sheet** | Judged against our own DESIGN-SYSTEM §12 this fails regardless of what they do. **A grabber that does not grab is a lie the hand discovers instantly** | S · medium |

Fix that and it is a genuine asset |


---

## What we made too complicated

**Worst offender: the wizard header.** Ten controls in one unwrappable flex row, so the brass **Next** sits 249 px off the right edge of a 390 px phone on **all ten steps** (`screens/Wizard.tsx:287-297`; measured live `header.scrollWidth 639 / clientWidth 390`). The engineer must scroll the document sideways to advance the wizard, every step, every project — and Save, Home, Help and the m/ft toggle are off-screen with it.

**The theme in most of the 83 rows below:** the control is a *mode* you must go find — behind a radial ring, inside a modal sheet, or in a `title=` a finger can never trigger — and the panel that holds it is a fixed-width desktop box drawn on top of the thing you are editing. ResLink's answer is the same nearly everywhere: **the action rides on the object, and the mode you need is already on.**

*104 `tooComplicated` entries from thirteen dimensions, deduplicated and merged to 83 rows. Ranked by pain removed for an EPC engineer standing on a roof with a phone — rows 1–35 are field-blocking, rows 36–65 are field-annoying, rows 66–83 are desk-side. All paths relative to `src/features/solar-studio/`.*

| # | What's hard today | Our flow now (taps/clicks) | How ResLink does it | Fix in (file:line) | Effort |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **Pressing Next.** Ten controls, one unwrappable flex row, no width media query | Scroll the *document* 249 px sideways on every one of 10 steps. Also off-screen: ft toggle (right 395), Save (401→441), Home (447→487), Help (493→533). Title wraps to 4 lines, eating 101 px of 780. Control heights 30–40 px — ten controls, ten failures of the 44 px rule | Six-item header everywhere: `Go back`, `Save project`, `Save & go home`, `Help`, `Upgrade`, `Next Step`/`Finish`, under a 4-tab bottom nav | `screens/Wizard.tsx:287-297`, `:308-311` — Back · truncated title · Next; Undo/Redo into each canvas toolbar; Save/Home/Help/units into one overflow | S |

Step 6: `onCanvasDown` returns `true` on every branch (`:1298-1323`) — the only way to pan is to switch the **ruler** on (`:1288`). Add the ~4 px drag threshold `three/Scene3D.tsx:3078` already uses | M |

### The eleven one-liners

If nothing else is done this week, these eleven changes remove a measurable amount of field pain for a few hours of work, and every one of them is a single value or a single expression.

| Row | Change | What it buys |
|---|---|---|
| 4 | `screens/Step3Obstructions.tsx:171` — delete `setPlacing(null)` | 24 taps → 10 for a six-object survey |
| 9 | `screens/Step3Obstructions.tsx:295` — `"mesh"` → `"map"` | Step 3's 3D view gets back the gizmo, the measure tools, the sun, the time bar and the neighbourhood |
| 10 | `screens/Step6Editor.tsx:805-809` — narrow the guard to `st.manual` | Manual stringing on a phone starts working at all |
| 11 | `theme.css:72-75` — 14 px → 16 px | Every number field in the studio stops zooming the iOS viewport |
| 16 | `screens/Step2Roof.tsx:154` — `useState(true)` | Rectilinear roofs trace square by default |
| 32 | `screens/Step6Editor.tsx:949-956` — clear only on success | 20 taps of hand-wiring stop vanishing on a refusal |
| 38 | `three/Scene3D.tsx:1190-1198, 1201-1207, 1209-1216` — drop the `!` | Toggles stop lying about their own state (and `aria-pressed` becomes correct) |
| 70 | `screens/Step6Editor.tsx:1056`, `three/Scene3D.tsx:1144` | One name for the heatmap instead of four |
| 76 | `screens/Step4Components.tsx:218, 840` — not `banner-warn` | Advice stops looking like an error |
| 82 | `screens/Step5AutoDesign.tsx:50` — route through `go()` | Resume lands where the user actually was |
| 65 | `lib/bom.ts:116` — threshold instead of `> 0` | PRELIMINARY starts meaning something |


---

## Bugs and broken paths

Thirteen agents read our code against ResLink's bundle and returned **201 defects**. Deduplicated
(several dimensions found the same defect from different angles) they are **176 distinct bugs**.
Every gate is green while all of them ship. Read the two lead tables first; everything after them
is the full register, grouped by what it costs.

---

### Read this first: the four money bugs

These four put a wrong number or a false statement on a document that leaves the building.

| # | Bug | Evidence (file:line) | What it costs | Fix |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| M1 | **A negotiated discount never reaches the customer's price.** `designFp` serialises `pricing.marginPct` and not `pricing.discount`, so the memo key does not change when a discount is entered and the warm cache is returned | `lib/fingerprints.ts:183`; `types.ts:996`; `lib/derive/outputs.ts:27-37`; 4-entry LRU at `lib/derive/memo.ts:13-25`. **Reproduced live** on the 8-panel fixture: cached `{total 276871, discount 0}` vs true `{total 249183, discount 26136}` | **₹27,688 overcharge on the fixture.** The CSV is right (`lib/bom.ts:165-167` recomputes) and the printed proposal is wrong — from one design. Breaks "money never renders while stale" | **S** — add one field to the fingerprint |
| M2 | **A hardcoded AC cable size, wrong on 24 of 24 three-phase catalogue combinations.** A bare literal sitting between three correctly-derived siblings | `lib/sld.ts:52` `acCableSizeMm2: inv.phases === 3 ? 10 : 6`. Printed at `screens/Step8Sld.tsx:496,502` (DISCOM sheet) and `screens/ProposalView.tsx:499` (customer). BOM uses `sizeAcCable` (`lib/bom/emitters/electrical.ts:47`). Executed over the catalogue: Amaze 30 kVA ×4 = 120 kW → MCCB 250 A, sheet prints 10 mm², derived 150 mm². 10 mm² carries 55 A | **A cable that cannot carry the current, on the DISCOM submission and the customer's spec table.** `lib/sld.ts:16` claims "the sheet and the quote can never print two different ratings" | **S** — call `sizeAcCable` |
| M3 | **A printable GST-inclusive quote can be issued at the default 12 % margin the EPC never saw** — from Step 7, skipping Steps 8 and 9; and `/proposal` is reachable with no wizard at all | `screens/Step7Proposal.tsx:366-376` `patch({status:'proposal_ready'}); navigate('/proposal')`. `screens/Wizard.tsx:111-140` gates steps 1, 2, 4, 6 only. **`router.ts:49-51` exposes `/proposal` and `/bom` as ungated top-level routes** and **`screens/Dashboard.tsx:278`** navigates straight to `/proposal` from the row menu, so `allowedStep` never runs. `DEFAULT_MARGIN_PCT = 12` at `data/pricebook.ts:152`, applied at `store/store.tsx:142` | **A quote leaves at a margin nobody chose.** `lib/review.ts:42-161` produces four readiness items and not one concerns price or margin | **S** — gate the button, gate the route |
| M4 | **The layout sheet asserts "PV TABLE SECTION (10° TILT)" and "HDG steel legs · chemical anchor" regardless of the design** — six fixed line coordinates reading no project data | `screens/Step8Sld.tsx:724-733`; repeated in the SLD dimension at `:726` | A flush monorail on a 15° tin shed with sheet standoffs still prints a 10° tilted table on chemical anchors. **The contractor builds from this sheet.** ResLink's `Typical Table Arrangement` carries the real tilt, clearance, leg height and standoff height | **M** — derive from `deriveStructures` |

**Three cross-cutting findings each appeared in only one dimension and are easy to lose.** They are
marked ⚑ in the tables below: `store/useTmySync.ts:15-16` (one failed fetch pins the session to the
weaker engine), `components/EnergyReportSheet.tsx:23-26` (bypasses the memos and gates on the wrong
freshness answer), `lib/ops/electrical-ops.ts:145-149` (silently re-points strings at different
physical inverters — corrupts the SLD, the DXF and the installation sheet).

customer, a DISCOM or a supplier reads.

---

### Blocks the user

Forty-four defects that stop the job on the device the product is sold for.

| Bug | Evidence (file:line) | What the user sees | Severity |
|---|---|---|---|
| **A false `mppt_overflow` permanently blocks the wizard on ordinary 15–25 kW C&I designs** | `lib/stringing.ts:184-191` compares `strings.length` against `mppt.count * inverterCount`, ignoring `parallelPerMppt` — the rule the planner itself uses (`lib/electrical/balance.ts:99-108`). Executed live: 40 × Adani 540 on a Growatt MID 20KTL3-X (4 legal slots) → 3 legal strings, then `error mppt_overflow: "3 strings exceed available 2 MPPT inputs"`. Reproduced on Sungrow SG15RT, Growatt MID 20KTL3-X and Solis S5-GC25K. `screens/Wizard.tsx:113-136,209-217` caps `allowedStep` at 6 | Step 8 cannot be opened at all. The user is force-navigated back with a toast that names no fix | **critical** |
| **Manual stringing in 2D refuses every tap on any auto-strung design — which is every design** | `screens/Step6Editor.tsx:805-809` matches AUTO string owners too; `store/useElectricalSync.ts:23-34` re-derives the auto plan on a 150 ms debounce so every enabled module always belongs to a string. The 3D handler has no such guard (`three/Scene3D.tsx:2365`) and the op refuses only `s.manual` owners (`lib/ops/electrical-ops.ts:55`) | The phone gets the broken half of the feature | **critical** |
| **The 2D design canvas covers ~90 m of ground and runs at half the imagery resolution the 3D uses** | `components/SatCanvas.tsx:104-120,236-237` — `SAT_ZOOM 20`, 640 px, `scale` default 1 (`lib/maps.ts:9,33-42`). 89.8 m at 20°N, 83.9 m Delhi, 93.1 m Chennai. `viewBox="0 0 1000 1000"` (`:250-254`) clips beyond ±46 m. `three/Scene3D.tsx:2609-2610` fetches the same tile at `scale=2` and calls `zoomCovering()` (`lib/maps.ts:56-66`), which SatCanvas never imports | A 1 MWp ground mount (~127 m square with setbacks) cannot be traced or even seen. The surface you DESIGN on has a quarter of the pixels of the one you LOOK at | **critical** |
| **The Next button renders 240 px off-screen on every one of the ten steps at 390 px** | Verified live at 390×780 on `3700f6b`: `clientWidth 390`, `header.scrollWidth 630`, Next right edge 630 (`offBy: 240`). Also off-screen: ft toggle (395), Save project (441), Save & go home (487), Help (533). Cause `screens/Wizard.tsx:287-296` — flex, `space-between`, no `flexWrap`; one width media query exists in the whole repo (`theme.css:1165`) and is unrelated. Header measured 101 px because the title wraps to four lines (`:307-309`) | The wizard's primary action is unreachable on a phone | **critical** |
| **"Delete Roof 1" is physically unreachable** | Verified live at 390 px: roof-chip list (`screens/Step2Roof.tsx:1624-1633`) spans 12→206; the selected-roof rail (`:1700-1706`) spans 179→376. **27 px of overlap, both `z-index:30`.** `elementFromPoint` at the button centre returns `DIV.tool-rail`. Lock and Delete both measure **25×15 px** (375 px² against 1936 required) | The roof cannot be deleted, and DOM order decides which control wins | **critical** |
| **Zero touch-gesture code on the 2D canvas — it cannot be panned or zoomed by finger** | `grep -rnE 'onTouchStart\|onTouchMove\|\.touches\|pinch\|navigator\.vibrate' src` → 2 hits, both prose comments. `components/SatCanvas.tsx:233` `touchAction:'none'`; `:152` pan requires middle mouse or `panEnabled`; `:180-196` zoom is wheel-only plus two 38 px buttons; `dragRef` (`:97`) tracks one pointer so pinch is impossible by construction. In Step 6 every branch of `onCanvasDown` returns `true` (`screens/Step6Editor.tsx:1287-1322`) so `mode` is always `'custom'` and `panEnabled` (`:1285`) is never consulted; the only exception is the Measure ruler (`:1288`). In Step 2, `panEnabled={!draft…}` (`:1250`) blocks panning while tracing | A shed wider than the viewport cannot be traced. The whole "design on the roof" claim rests on this surface | **critical** |
| **With a roof selected, a one-finger drag inside it MOVES THE ROOF instead of panning** | `screens/Step2Roof.tsx:2834-2842` — the selected roof's `<path>` calls `stopPropagation` + `setPointerCapture` + `onBodyDown`, so `SatCanvas.down()` never runs (`components/SatCanvas.tsx:145-166`). `finishRoof` auto-selects the new roof (`:790`), which usually fills the screen | Immediately after tracing, panning is impossible until bare canvas is found to deselect on — and with no pinch, no way to zoom out to find any | **critical** |
| **96–100 % of interactive controls fail the 44 px minimum on every measured screen** | Measured live at 390×780: Step 2 → 27 of 28; Step 6 → 26 of 27; **Step 9 → 234 of 234**. Roots in `theme.css`: `.btn` 40 (`:110`), `.chip` 30 (`:251`), `.toggle` 42×24 (`:563-566`), `.tool-btn` 38×38 (`:621-623`), `.ctx-btn` 28 (`:741-743`), `.step-btn` 32×32 (`:534-536`), `.field input` 40 (`:189`). Also bare `<input type="checkbox">` for DCR and ALMM (`screens/Step4Components.tsx:734-739`). Smallest: 25×15 (Lock/Delete Roof), 9×9 and 7×7 (halo hubs folded). The `.tap-target` utility written for this (`src/design/index.css:190-200`) is used **zero times** | Nothing on a phone is reliably tappable | **critical** |
| **Loading, empty, error and offline states do not exist anywhere; there are zero error boundaries** | `grep -rni aria-busy` → 0; `grep ErrorBoundary\|componentDidCatch` → 0 outside `three/ObstructionMesh.tsx:41` (a GLB fallback); `grep onLine` → 0; no skeleton component; `src/app/(studio)/StudioClientLayout.tsx:143` returns `hydrated ? children : null` — a blank first paint on every route; `components/SatCanvas.tsx:236-250` renders the satellite base with **no `onError`**. `find src/app -name 'error*' -o -name 'loading*'` → nothing | One throw blanks the product. A quota failure on a rooftop renders a silent dark rectangle the user then traces a roof onto. ResLink ships a named `Bill of Materials` boundary with recovery copy and a Skip button | **critical** |
| **3D box-select requires the Shift key** | `three/Scene3D.tsx:526` `if (!e.shiftKey \|\| e.button !== 0) return;` gates the whole marquee; Shift-table wiring at `:2358-2364` is gated the same way | On a phone exactly one module can ever be selected in 3D | **critical** |
| **Every keystroke in Target Capacity synchronously re-runs six complete candidate designs** | `screens/Step4Components.tsx:168-170` writes `targetKwp` on every `onChange`; it is inside `electricalFp` → `designFp` (`lib/fingerprints.ts:141,161-163`), which keys `memoizedComparison` (`lib/comparison.ts:426-433`). Called on every render, not only when the sheet opens (`Step4Components.tsx:113-118`). Each miss runs 6 candidates × [`buildCandidateProject` twice, `computeEnergyReport` (8760-hour when TMY is loaded), `computeFinancials`, `validateSystem`] | Typing "15" on a 200-module C&I roof runs twelve full multi-roof fills, six auto-stringings, six energy simulations, six BOMs and six financial models on the main thread — twice | high |
| **Nudging one module by a centimetre throws away the 12-month roof heatmap and re-runs ~540,000 raycasts, twice** | Both heatmaps cache on `shadingFp` (`three/Scene3D.tsx:791`, `screens/Step6Editor.tsx:393`), which serialises every panel centre to cm (`lib/fingerprints.ts:290-318`), while `computeHeatmap` has no panel dependency at all. The correct key already exists: `geometryFp` (`lib/fingerprints.ts:43-73`). `heatCacheRef` is a one-entry ref declared separately per view (`three/Scene3D.tsx:750`, `Step6Editor.tsx:~307`) so 2D→3D recomputes from scratch | The shading review freezes on every nudge, and again on every view switch | high |
| **Resume lands on the wrong step: `wizardStep` is a high-water mark that Back also raises** | `screens/Wizard.tsx:242-245` `patch({ wizardStep: Math.max(project.wizardStep, clamped) })`, and `onBack()` (`:258-260`) calls the same `go()`. `screens/Dashboard.tsx:97-100` resumes there. Five sites bypass `go()` and desynchronise it further: `Step5AutoDesign.tsx:50`, `Step7Proposal.tsx:222`, `Step8Sld.tsx:314`, `Step10Done.tsx:64`, `Dashboard.tsx:279-280` | Walk back from Step 9 to Step 2 to retrace a roof, reopen from the dashboard, and land on "SLD & Drawings" | high |

### Wrong engineering answer

Fifty-nine defects where the number, the geometry or the electrical verdict is wrong. Most never
surface as an error.

| Bug | Evidence (file:line) | What the user sees | Severity |
|---|---|---|---|
| **The solar-access heatmap silently ignores neighbouring buildings** | `lib/solar-heatmap.ts:237` `buildShadowCasters(project)` with no options; `lib/scene-model.ts:189,309` gates the DSM block on `opts.surround`. `computeHeatmap`'s opts type (`:194-202`) has no `surround` field and neither call site could pass one (`three/Scene3D.tsx:809-813`, `screens/Step6Editor.tsx:402`). `lib/shading.ts:208-211` and `store/useDesignSync.ts:50-52` do pass it | On any urban site: modules render red at 78 % access from a G+4 next door while the heatmap cell underneath renders green at 96 %, in the same frame, both labelled "Solar access". The heatmap is the one that reaches the customer. (Secondary: the heatmap's eye is 0.45 m above the deck, `lib/solar-heatmap.ts:111`, while the engine's rays leave the module plane, `lib/shading.ts:155`) | **critical** |
| **The auto-design roof ranking ignores the neighbourhood entirely** | `lib/auto-design.ts:90` `computeSolarAccess({ ...project, panels: probe })` — no second argument, so `surround` is undefined. Every other caller passes it (`lib/analysis-client.ts:75`, `workers/analysis.worker.ts:34`, fed by `store/useDesignSync.ts:51-52`) | A G+4 block 9 m south: the DSM has it, the energy engine shades with it, and the auto-designer ranks that roof as fully open — then fills it first and prints "measured sun access (98 %)" on Step 5 and, via `lib/proposal-narrative.ts:98-107`, on the customer proposal | **critical** |
| **A target-kWp auto-design places the WORST panels on the roof** — the budget truncates in raw lattice order | `lib/layout.ts:398` returns from inside the column loop, so the kept panels are the first N row-major from the region's min corner; `lib/auto-design.ts:182` passes `maxPanels: remaining`. **Verified** on a 24×30 m Pune roof with a 12 m block 1 m off the south edge, 6 kWp target: all 11 panels landed in one row at y=−13.6; mean measured access **58.7 %** where the best eleven measured **100.0 %**. The log printed "190 panels possible · 91 % sun access" | ~40 % of the annual yield thrown away by the button the product is sold on, while the explanation screen says it chose by measured sun access | **critical** |
| **The shadow frustum is fitted to roofs and panels only, so a tall obstruction outside that box casts no shadow in the picture while the engine counts it in the number** | `three/scene-bounds.ts:22-56` reads `roofs[].polygon` and `project.panels` and nothing else; `three/Scene3D.tsx:756,2719` sizes the only shadow camera from it. Worked example: a 10×12 m house → `shadowHalf` 20.25 m; a 12 m building 25 m south is outside the box and draws no shadow, while `lib/shading.ts` raycasts it correctly | The customer is shown a sunlit roof beside a number that says it is shaded. Directly contradicts "the scene and the model agree" | **critical** |
| ⚑ **Placing one inverter over the count silently deletes the OLDEST and shifts every remaining index, re-pointing existing strings at physically different boxes** | `lib/ops/electrical-ops.ts:145-149` `next.slice(-count)`; same shape on delete at `:187`. `PvString.inverterIndex` is positional (`types.ts:735`), resolved at `lib/routing.ts:478-479`, and nothing remaps it. The `assumedTarget` honesty flag (`lib/routing.ts:497`) fires only for out-of-range. Those indices print at `screens/Step8Sld.tsx:576`, `lib/export-dxf.ts:115` and `screens/CableScheduleSheet.tsx:50`. The 2D hint tells the user it is safe: "tap an edge to move the oldest one" (`screens/Step6Editor.tsx:1727`) | A string wired to INV 1 now terminates on a different box — on the SLD, the DXF and the sheet the crew pulls cable from | high |
| ⚑ **Two entirely different energy engines, chosen by whether one network fetch succeeded, with no marker on the customer document** | `store/useTmySync.ts:15-16` declares module-scope `skippedWeather`/`skippedTmy` Sets; the only mutations are `.add` at `:53` and `:95` — **no delete, no clear, no retry**. `lib/energy/report.ts:102-103` runs the hourly engine only `if (tmyYear)`; the monthly branch returns the same `irradianceSource: 'PVGIS'` (`:223`) with 5 constants including a fixed 8.1 % temperature loss for all of India (`lib/solar.ts:50`) against the hourly branch's 11 measured losses. `r.engine` is read in exactly one place (`components/EnergyReportSheet.tsx:101`) | One failed PVGIS call on rooftop 4G pins the whole session to the weaker engine, and the customer cannot tell an 8760-hour simulation from a rule of thumb | high |
| **The isotropic sky-view factor uses 1 − sin β where the physics is cos²β** | `lib/energy/hourly.ts:439-440`. At 30° the truth is 0.75 and this returns 0.50; at 15°, 0.933 vs 0.741. It multiplies the whole isotropic diffuse term (`:300,303`) and the rear-side irradiance (`:331`). The 0.3 floor means a courtyard site saturates and stops responding to geometry | In monsoon India diffuse is 50–70 % of the resource, so this is a first-order annual error on every obstructed site | high |

---

### Cosmetic / polish

Twenty-one defects that change no number but decide whether the product reads as finished. The 3D
rows carry their dimension's own P-ORDER, which is the recommended build order for the
hyperrealism ask.

| Bug | Evidence (file:line) | What the user sees | Severity |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Every shadow has the same blur** — PCF applies one fixed kernel regardless of occluder distance | `three/Scene3D.tsx:1401` `shadows={{type: THREE.PCFSoftShadowMap}}`; `grep 'SoftShadows\|AccumulativeShadows\|ContactShadows' src` → **zero**. drei 10.7.7 (`package.json:17`) ships `<SoftShadows/>` (PCSS) — a one-component addition | A 17 m tree edge and a 35 mm module frame edge are equally crisp: the fastest "this is CG" tell there is. **P-ORDER 4a, effort S** | high |
| **Peter-panning: a 30 mm shadow normalBias on a 45 mm module** | `three/Scene3D.tsx:2795,2846` `shadow-normalBias={0.03}`; the glass box is 45 mm (`three/PanelsInstanced.tsx:145`) and the frame under it 20 mm (`:164`). N8AO, which would darken that crease, is off on every mobile device (`three/ScenePost.tsx:20-25`) | A visible ribbon of light under every module edge and every leg base — the array reads as floating. **P-ORDER 4b, effort S** | high |
| **The IBL reflects three white slabs, not the sky, so glass had to be crippled to hide it** | `three/Scene3D.tsx:2746-2770` — `<Environment resolution={128} frames={1}>` with three `<Lightformer form="rect">`; the live `<Sky>` (`:2801-2808`) and the satellite ground are outside it. The symptom is documented in code: `three/textures.ts:220-222` → `envMapIntensity: 0.2`, while the frame stays at 1 (`:200`) | Every reflective surface mirrors a featureless rectangle; frames read as grey plastic, not anodised aluminium. **P-ORDER 3, effort S** | high |
| **The sun's brightness saturates at 14.3° altitude and its colour never changes** | `three/Scene3D.tsx:2680` `duskFactor = min(1, max(0, sunAltitude/0.25))` — radians, so 0.25 rad = 14.3°; `:2834` intensity is a flat 1.75 for the rest of the day; `:2835` `color="#fff4e0"` is constant while `<Sky>` (`:2801-2808`) correctly reddens; `:2679` `sunVisible = sunAltitude > 0` swaps Sky for Stars in one frame; `:2822` fog is a fixed `#b9c7d8` | On the screen sold as "Real-Time Sun Path Simulation": 10:00, noon and 16:00 are lit identically, there is no sunrise, no sunset, no twilight, and at 17:30 the sky is orange while the roof is midday cream. **P-ORDER 5, effort S** | high |

The module is one box with the cell-face texture on all six faces (`three/PanelsInstanced.tsx:145`) | 500 pixel-identical, impossibly clean modules in the country where soiling is the most visible rooftop condition — and from below in walkthrough the module's BACK shows cells. **P-ORDER 11, effort M** | high |

No LOD, no billboard, no instancing: five trees is 5.7 M triangles rendered twice per frame at 34.7 MB of shared geometry | The clone-army tell, on the object Indian rooftops have most. **P-ORDER 13, effort M-L** | medium |

The verification surface is broken | low |
